# Kingsroad Keep

The original medieval tower-defense game: a classic board, waves of foes, towers, heroes and scrolls.
It is one self-contained page (Three.js is loaded from cdnjs, fonts from Google Fonts).

| | |
|---|---|
| Source | `src/kingsroad-keep.html` (includes the local-test hooks between `//LT-BEGIN` and `//LT-END`) |
| Build | `python build.py` -> `dist/kingsroad-keep.html` (hooks stripped, pure ASCII) |
| Dev server | `python -m http.server 8771` from this folder and open `/src/kingsroad-keep.html`; `__kk.ev(code)` runs code inside the game |
| Tests | `tests/classic-smoke.js`, `tests/resolve-test.js`, `tests/spells-test.js` (`__st.damage([...])` damage per cast at P 100, `__st.combos()` fires every new synergy) (run with `__kk.ev(await (await fetch('/tests/<file>')).text())` on the dev page) |
| Headless (cloud) | `node tests/headless.js` runs every suite through run-all (about 8 minutes), `node tests/headless.js mpg gsm` only those (`ALL=1` lists passing rows too), in headless Chromium (needs Playwright; the dev server must be running). Where cdnjs is blocked, `npm i --no-save three@0.128.0` first and Three.js r128 is served from `node_modules`. Every suite passes on a brand-new browser profile (since v397) |
| Visual checks | `tests/visual-shots.js`: `await __save(spell, [times], {focus, zoom})` renders 4 moments of a cast into one sheet and saves it through `python _backup/shot_srv.py 8766` (works with the Browser pane hidden) |
| Balance sims | `tests/balance-sim.js` loads `__bal`: `queue(...)`/`step(ms)` for whole bot runs, `lane(cfg)` for how long unkillable foes are held, `value(cfg)` for damage at equal gold. Old builds to compare against live in `_backup/` |
| Identity | `GAME = {id:'keep', key:'kingsroad'}` near the top of the script |
| Storage | `kingsroad.meta`, `kingsroad.run`, `kingsroad.accounts` (unchanged from before the split, so every existing save keeps working) |

## What the split changed here
* Always the classic board. The open-world switch is hidden and forced off.
* A saved open-world run is **set aside, not deleted** (`kingsroad.run.open`, with a timestamp suffix if one already exists) and is never resumed here.
* More > "Kingsgaard transfer": a `KK1:` code with this game's heroes and spells plus the set-aside open-world run, to paste into Kingsgaard (Load > Import a save code).
* Settings no longer show the open-world only options (render distance, shadow distance, mouse sensitivity, foliage).

## Resolve (crowd control)
Every stun, root, daze and push-back from buildings, hero attacks, synergies and spells below legendary goes through `stunE(e,sec)` / `pushE(e,units)`
(next to `moveEnemy`). A foe can be locked for `RSV.cap` = 2 s in total (bosses 0.6 s; a push costs distance / speed seconds), then it is Unstoppable for
4 s (bosses 8 s): gold ring, "Unstoppable!" label, no stuns or pushes, slows still work. Legendary, mythic, ancient and unique spells still set
`e.stunT` / `e.dist` directly and ignore Resolve. New crowd control must use the two helpers, or it can bring back endless stun-locks.

## Elemental scrolls (batch 1)
Six scrolls fill the missing elements at low rarity: Frost Shard (ice), Static Spark (lightning), Cinder Scatter (fire) are common; Rime Bloom (ice),
Ball Lightning (lightning), Sunflare (light) are uncommon. Their code sits in one block just before the scroll art is built (search `Elemental scrolls batch 1`),
and it hooks in by wrapping `castScrollNow2`, `comboReact`, `hurt`, `statusTick`, `comboPrep` and `comboTick`. New status: Charged (`e.zapT`). New
synergies: Shatterfrost, Overload, Conductor, Flashpoint, Frostbite, Supercell, Sunfire (in `COMBOS`, so they show in the Cookbook).

Batch 2 (search `Scrolls batch 2`) adds Ice Tomb, Magma Geyser, Murder of Crows (rare) and Rite of the Phoenix, Frostfall Comet, Thunder Wyrm (epic),
plus the Ashen Flock and Absolute Zero synergies. Shared helpers there: `nsTrail` (camera-facing ribbon from a position history), `nsSeams` (glowing
ground cracks), `nsLead` (aim a late-landing strike where the foe will be). Patch notes go into the current notes version (`secrets-1` since the reset of 2026-10-20).

Batch 3 (search `Legendary scrolls batch 3`) adds Skyfall, Glacial Rift and Grim Harvest. The reaper (`nsReaper`) wears a torn cloak made with
`nsStrips` (one merged strip geometry whose vertices sway every frame, vertex-coloured to frayed tips), swings on an `nsTrack` keyframe curve (cubic
Hermite, no kinks), rises on the road ahead of the strongest foe (`nsStrong`: bosses, then max HP) facing up the path, and re-aims the second sweep if
the first killed its mark. Its halo is placed every frame behind the head as the camera sees it. Glacial Rift adds `nsBlizzard` (snow and fog over the
rift, `nsFrostVeil` screen-edge frost, removed by `resetCine`) and `nsFreezeFoe` (a translucent ice block on each foe the wave hits). Anything a scroll adds to the scene outside its own effect
object must go through `nsOwn(obj)` / `nsFree(obj)`: `resetCine()` then removes it when a battle ends or is left mid-cast (plain `scene.add` leaves it stuck).

## Mythic scrolls (batch 5)
Search `Mythic scrolls batch 5` (the block just before the scroll art is built; source in `_backup/mythic_b.js` + `mythic_data.js`, applied by
`_backup/p_mythic.py` on top of v284; since v287 `_backup/p_spacecalm.py` goes on after it). Measured with `__st.walk` and a 24-foe column (`_backup/spread_test.js`, `__st.spread`) they sit with the
older mythics (Vael'Thoruun, Eclipse): one / pack of 8 / column of 24 at P 100.
* **Wrath of the Ember Titan** (fire, cd 80; about 3900 / 31700 / 36500 since v286: blade 2200%, tip blast 1000% within 3). `myTitanSpot` scores road spots behind the pack (facing on)
  and ahead of it (facing back) by the foes the blade will land on when it falls, and on a tie faces the camera so its body does not hide the blow.
  The greatsword is built by `bladeGeo` (hexagonal cross-section; molten rims and a lava strip are slightly larger copies that only show past the
  iron). The fissure is `myRoadSeams`, a copy of `nsPathSeams` that grows from the blade outwards in either direction along the road.
  The blow is `myBlast`: `myFireball` (a noise-displaced sphere shader that cools from white-hot to soot and fades), `myFireRing` (a thin
  additive ring racing over the ground), `myLavaRock` (thrown rocks with fire trails that splash where they land), a crater and smoke.
* **Eternal Winter** (ice, cd 110, global; about 1400 per foe, every foe on the map). The Heart lands on open ground next to the pack
  (`myHeartSpot`, so the frozen foes stay in sight). Since v288 it forms first: `vort` shards spiral in at `FY` = 5.2 over a frost sigil, it
  swells out of them (`formed` burst), dives (cubic ease-in) with an `nsTrail` comet tail, and lands at `LAND` = 1.6 (unchanged, so damage
  timing is the same) with icy cracks and `mkNova()`: two rings of spikes that skip road tiles and foes and stand until the shatter. The frost floor (`myFrostFloor`, one shader plane over the board) spreads from it (`uR`);
  each foe it reaches is frozen until the shatter wave (`uW`, 17 u/s) reaches it, then shatters for 1200%. The CSS aurora is `#myAurora`.
* **Lance of the Seraph** (light, cd 70; about 4500 / 25400 / 14000-16000). `mySeraph` is a real 3D figure (since v286): it turns to the
  camera once when cast (yaw, leaning back by half the camera's elevation) and then keeps that place, so turning the camera shows it
  from the side. Each of its six wings is a group at the shoulder (`wingG`) that sweeps back, opens and beats; its feathers hang from a
  bone that curves backwards and are turned about their own shafts. Feathers are normal-blended and write depth (additive feathers went
  white). Glows are sprites (`myGlowSprite`, right from any side), the rays are a 3D cone of crossed quads, the sigil is a circle beneath it.
Each one's main effect stops itself when `state` leaves 'playing' (game over mid-cast), and everything it adds sits under `nsOwn`.

## Calm space-bending scrolls (setting)
`meta.spaceCalm` (Settings window row, the More and pause menu toggles `#bSpace`/`#bSpaceP`; kept across account switches). Added by
`_backup/p_spacecalm.py` on top of v286. While it is on and a space-bending scroll runs (`SDP.n>0`, `SGL.n>0`, Infinite Void's `DOM.on`/post pass,
or `SPACE_T` for Wormhole), `spaceStep(dt)` eases `SPACE_K` to `SPACE_CALM` = .15, and that factor scales:
* the warp passes: `uK` in the SDP shader (Deflection, Loop, Compression, Shatter, Citadel, space quakes) and in Infinite Void's post pass;
  Compression's object squeeze (`comp()`) is scaled the same way so things keep their size;
* Singularity's lensing (`uLens`), darkness sphere, colour grade and collapse flare; Shatter's frozen-frame shards and crack;
* the screen flash, vignette, darkness (`da`) and camera shake for everything on screen while one runs;
* Infinite Void's white light flashes and slash blackouts. Its screen-wide slashes are drawn far away instead (same three hits).
Damage is identical either way (checked with `__st.damage` on and off). A new space-bending scroll must add itself to `spaceStep`'s test.

## Arenas, the world map and the wave 200 victory (v289, Arena 3 in v290)
Source: `_backup/worldmap_b.js` (the painted map) + `_backup/arena_b.js` / `arena_b2.js` / `arena_b3.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_arena.py`
just before the boot `buildMap(...)` line; then `_backup/tundra_b.js`, inserted after them by `_backup/p_tundra.py` (which also patches the arena
code for the third land; since v295 it also appends the sibling `_backup/lands_b.js`). Rebuild chain: v284 + `p_mythic.py` + `p_spacecalm.py` + `p_arena.py` (= v289)
+ `p_tundra.py` + `p_rewards.py` (+ `p_crowns.py` + `p_mass.py` = v298, + `p_bosses.py` = v299, + `p_polish.py` = v300, + `p_ancient.py` (takes the `_backup` folder) = v301/v302 (the v301 modules are kept as `ancient_*.v301.js`, `p_ancient.v301.py`), + `p_balance.py` = v303/v304, + `p_fixes.py` = v305/v306; earlier tundra modules are kept as `tundra_b.v290.js` / `v292` / `v294` with their `p_tundra.v2xx.py`).
Tests: `tests/arena-test.js` (152 checks). Frame sheets: `tests/tundra-shots.js`
(`__tun.go(arena)`, `__tun.foes([...])`, `__tun.units()`, `__tun.gust(k)`, `__tun.props(arena)` (the land's own lore props on free spots, returns the spots), the standard views `__tun.V.day/night/units`, `await __tun.sheet(name,[view code...],cols,{grey:true})`
for a greyscale squint copy, saved through `shot_srv.py` 8766). Before/after sheets: `_backup/shots/tun-compare*.jpg` (v291 -> v292 readability) and `tun-autumn-compare*.jpg` (v292 -> v293 autumn).
* **State** (in meta, kept per account, defaulted by `arenaFix` through a `migrateMeta` wrapper): `arena` (where the keep is), `arenas` (open lands;
  Frostvale and the tundra open together), `seenA` (unlock shown), `bestA` (best wave per arena: the start chips and Best wave
  use it), `wonA`, `mapIntro` (the unlock still has to play). An old save already past wave 200 gets the lands and the intro. Runs carry `r.arena`
  and `r.v200`; `loadRun` switches to the run's arena first. Travelling ends a saved run (`settleSavedRun`, crowns kept). An `ARENAS` entry with
  `soon:true` is drawn on the map but never playable (none since v290). The world map button shows a badge while an open land is unvisited (`arenaUnseen`).
* **Wave 200** is now the Ophanim (`FIXED_BOSS`), the Wyrmfiend takes 250-450 and the Ophanim its `BOSS_LATE` slot. `waveLocked` adds `v200Lock()`
  so 201 cannot start until 200 is decided; `checkWaves` calls `arenaWin()` (fireworks, `#vict`: leave = `gameOver` titled Victory, or fight on).
  Winning in the Plains opens Frostvale and the tundra and sets `mapIntro`.
* **World map** (`openWorldMap(intro)`): `wmpPaint()` paints a 1000x1400 parchment canvas once (noise coast with bays and isles, watercolour
  biome washes, ink mountains/forests/tufts, roads as Catmull-Rom paths in `WMP.paths`, lakes, compass, ship, names) in about 0.5 s and caches it.
  Keeps are DOM buttons over it (`wmapSites`, states cur/open/new/soon/locked/far). Travel (`wmapTravel`) drives an SVG carriage along the road;
  a first visit breaks the lock (`wmapArrive`), then `arenaGoNow` rebuilds the board. It opens by itself on the home screen while `mapIntro` is set
  and there is no saved run. Names here use the `wmap`/`wmp-` prefix: the merchant owns `wm*` (a clash once broke him).
* **Frostvale board** (`buildMap` wrapper then `snowBoard()`): tiles recoloured, materials swapped by `SNOW_REMAP`, snow on spruce tiers and roofs by
  `snowTopMat` (object-space top of each cone) and on rocks by `snowMat`, drifts and icicles as two instanced meshes (no extra draw calls).
  `arenaLight` swaps the day/dusk/night palettes; `snowAtmos` (wrapped into `sfAtmos`) makes it snow; `arenaPV()` colours the home preview.
* **Frostvale foes**: in `spawnEnemy`, +15% HP (bosses +25%) and +10% speed, `en.snow`, then `snowify(en.b)` (each material swapped for a cached
  snowy twin: white on faces that look up, a cold tint, a frost rim; glows and additive parts untouched; the shared materials never change) and
  `snowGear` (a fur collar from the arm joints). Not the Ophanim or the Seraphim. Frozen breath comes from a `moveEnemy` wrapper.
* **Arena 3, the Greywind Tundra** (v290; readable in v292; an autumn tundra since v293, search `Arena 3: the Greywind Tundra`): opens with Frostvale; the first trip plays the lock break.
  * Readability order (Simon's briefs): units and foes first, then the road, then the buildable tiles; decoration is low contrast on the board and
    lives mostly outside the grid. No snow here (Frostvale is the snow land).
  * Board (`tundraBoard()`): buildable tiles one muted rust-red moss colour (`TUN_C.moss`, +-3% per tile); `tunGround` (board variant) adds soft gold
    and olive patches at the moss's own brightness and +-4% mottle, so the board stays within about 8%. The road is lighter cool grey gravel
    (`TUN_C.path`) with mottle and faint slab joints (told apart by its lower tile top, y < -.012); tile sides are darkened so the gaps read as faint
    seams under the low sun. Low shrubs (`edge` instanced mesh) creep a little onto both road edges, clear of the foes' lane. Board rocks keep their
    colour with pale lichen spots (`tunLichenMat`, so spells that look for rocks by colour still find them), board trees are dark spruce. Pebbles and
    grass tufts are hidden (`TUN_HIDE`), the old trees around the board too. Since v295 the tundra has its own lore props (below), made clean;
    `tunLoreTidy` (old props lose their scorch/mud decals and specks) skips them. Spell craters and scorch marks vanish with their effects.
  * Inside the grid only rare faint details (`TUND.inner`, under 10% of free tiles, never on or beside the road): small muted shrubs, hidden under a
    new tower (`tunDecoHide` from a `placeTower` wrapper).
  * Border, the postcard zone (6 instanced meshes in all, `userData.tun`): dense crimson and gold shrub patches (`shrub`, `tunClumpGeo`) and a band
    along the board's foot, golden tussocks that sway (`tuft`), granite boulders and flat slabs with lichen (`rock`), a few dark spruce (`spruce`),
    icebergs (`berg`). The land itself is `tunGround` (around variant) on the big ground plane: full-colour carpet with gold, olive and red patches,
    a wet rock band at the shore and the sea off the left edge (`tunShore(z)` in JS, the same curve in GLSL), with a slow shimmer and foam.
  * The tile grid shows clearly only while placing (`tunGridMesh`, outlines of the free tiles, from `startPlacing`/`cancelPlacing` wrappers).
  * Light: `TUN_LIGHT` (low warm late-afternoon sun under a pale hazy blue sky, rose-amber dusk, cool moonlit night); a `dayStep` wrapper puts the sun
    half as high (long soft shadows); an `applyCam` wrapper pushes the fog out; `blobMat` is darker (.5) while the tundra is built and put back for
    the other lands; the hero gets a shadow disc too (`tunHeroBlob`), so a red-robed hero never blends into the red ground. `TUN_PV` is the home preview.
  * World map (v294, patched by `p_tundra.py`): the tundra is painted in autumn colours too (`WMP_PAL.tundra` rust and maroon washes with gold, crimson
    and gold shrub glyphs, olive tufts, granite-grey hills without snow caps, no blue cold-air streaks). Sheet: `_backup/shots/map-tundra-compare.jpg`.
  * Weather (`meta.weather` off/low/high, default low; `wxLevel`, `wxK`): a few drifting leaves in `tundraAtmos`, only over the border bands left and
    right of the board (never over the grid or its units), and the Frostvale snowfall (low = the old amount). Buttons `#bWeather` (More tab) / `#bWeatherP` (pause) and a Settings window row.
  * Foes (`spawnEnemy`): troops +20% HP, bosses +30% (`TUN.hpT/hpB`), normal speed, `en.tun`. The Greywind (`GUST`, `gustTick` in an `update`
    wrapper, game time) only counts down while a wave is on the field: every 25-35 s (start to start) a 1.5 s warning (`sfxGust` whistle and the
    Greywind sign `#tunPill`, `tunPillSync`), then a 4 s gust (leaves blow faster, tussocks lean: `TUNW.g` calm .3, warning .78, gust 1).
    `gustF(e)` multiplies the speed factor in `moveEnemy`: troops x1.4, bosses x1.2 at full strength (eased in .35 s, out .5 s). The first gust of a
    run shows a toast; `resetRun` resets the timer. Look: `tundrify` (material twins `kktun5`: a little more colour, a cool rim light, and at night
    a little light of their own via `TUNU.uTunN`) and `tunGear` (one mesh: a two-tone fur ruff, plus a short cape on troops; vertex colours).
    Not the Ophanim or the Seraphim. A faint breath puff from a `moveEnemy` wrapper.
  * Developer panel: Greywind gust (`gustNow()`: the next gust comes after its warning).
* **Each land's own lore props** (v295, `_backup/lands_b.js`, search `Land lore`): Frostvale has `frozen` (a frozen traveller), `sled`, `icehole`,
  `frostshrine`, `wolfkill` and `snowman`; the tundra `boat`, `whale` (bones), `cairn`, `fishrack`, `runestone` and `tent`. `LORE_SETS[arena]` picks
  the set (`loreKeys(out)`: each prop once before any repeats); the Plains keep `LORE_KEYS` and the very same placement (a test compares three seeds
  with v294). The notes are added to `LORE_D`, the radii to `LORE_SOLID`; `LORE_RULE[k]` holds the placement (`mn`/`mx` road distance, `face` the
  road, `big` needs more room), read by `placeLore` (patched in `p_tundra.py`). `bioModel` builds them (a `loreModel` wrapper) from their own
  materials (`bioM`, not the shared `M()` ones), so `snowBoard`/`tundraBoard` never recolour them; lichen is added on purpose (`bioL`), snow lies in
  separate white lumps (the snow shader turned them white from above). Cloth sways (`userData.flaps`, harder in a gust) and the rune stones glow
  brighter before and during a gust (`BIO.rune`), both from a `loreAnim` wrapper. Sheets: `_backup/shots/props-snow.jpg`, `props-tundra.jpg`, `props-boards.jpg`.
* **Breaking stones** (v295, same module): tap a board rock (after trees in `tap`, unless the hero stands in front) to open `rockOpen` (Old /
  Frozen / Granite Boulder by land); `ROCK_COST` 1000 gold breaks it (`breakRock`: it shudders .4 s, then chips, dust, a rumble). The tile becomes
  free (grid 2 -> 0). Each rock is two meshes found by geometry and offset after every `buildMap` (`rockIndex` -> `ROCKM`, works in every land
  whatever material the land gave it). Broken rocks go in `MAP.broke`, saved as `r.rocks` and broken again silently in `loadRun` (older saves have
  none). Sheet: `_backup/shots/stone-break.jpg`.
* **Up to three gates in the tundra** (v298, `gateN` + `MAP_GV` at the end of `lands_b.js`): `genMap` rolls one number for the gate count in every
  land, so the Plains and Frostvale boards are unchanged (one or two gates); in the tundra it opens one (about 22%), two (40%) or three (38%), still
  9 tiles apart. `MAP.gv` (board version 2) is saved with the run as `r.gv`; `loadRun` rebuilds a run without it under the old rule, so tundra
  runs saved before v298 keep their board and their towers stay off the road. Sheet: `_backup/shots/tundra-gates.jpg`.
* **The cold lands favour ice** (v296, end of `lands_b.js`, `LAND_EL`): a `hurt` wrapper multiplies ice scroll damage (`o.el==='ice'` and `o.spell`,
  so the frost tower's bolts do not count) by 1.2 in Frostvale and 1.1 in the tundra. Every slow is 15% stronger in both: `moveEnemy` passes the
  product of all its slows (`e.slow`, blizzards, domains, Purgatory, Chronostasis, Citadel; not the gust, which is a speed-up) through `landSlow(s)`
  = 1-(1-s)*1.15, never below 5% speed unless it already was (a stop stays a stop). Shown in the arrival window, the Bestiary toast and the notes.
* **Bestiary**: `#bGalBiome` cycles Plains > Frostvale > Tundra (`GAL_BIO`; `GAL_SNOW` is kept in step) and rebuilds the gallery (`galSnowM`, `galCol`).
* **Wyrmfiend**: `wyrmFiendX` adds magma cracks, a bone sail, membrane wings, a horn crown, a skull plate and a bladed tail, plus an ember `tick`.
* Developer tools: in a run, Jump to wave 200, New lands and Greywind gust; in the More tab (developer mode), a button that replays the map unlock.
* Adding a land: an `ARENAS` entry (and `SITE_ARENA`), a branch in `arenaNow`, the `buildMap` wrapper, `arenaLight`, `arenaPV`, `spawnEnemy`, the
  Bestiary cycle and `wmapArrive` (see `p_tundra.py` for the full list), a board dresser like `snowBoard`/`tundraBoard`. Check new top-level names
  against the base file; the merchant owns `wm*`.

## Harder lands pay better (v291)
Source: `_backup/rewards_b.js`, inserted just before the boot `buildMap(...)` line by `_backup/p_rewards.py` (which also patches the payout lines).
Rebuild chain: v290 + `p_rewards.py` (= v291, `_backup/kingsroad-keep.v291.html`). Tests: `tests/rewards-test.js` (18 checks).
* `ARENA_REWARD` per arena: `gem` (Plains 1, Frostvale 1.25, Greywind Tundra 1.5) and `rar` (0 / .04 / .08). `arenaReward()` reads it for `arenaNow()`.
* Gems: `arenaGems(n)` multiplies a payout and rolls the fraction (so it stays a whole number and the average is exact). Used on wave clears
  (the toast adds `arenaGemTag()`, e.g. "(Frostvale x1.25)"), Gem Carriers, the 60-gem treasure and treasure chests. Bestiary gems and trades are unchanged.
* Scrolls: every rarity above Common becomes (1+rar) times as likely, and Common gives up exactly that much, so the odds still add up to the same total.
  `rarP(r)` is the tilted chance (secret scrolls stay 0 outside the events); a `rollRarity` wrapper rolls with it, `rarTilt(WM_RW)` tilts the merchant's
  stock, and the scroll odds table shows `rarP` plus a line saying how much rarer it is in that land.
* Another land: just add its `ARENA_REWARD` entry (`n` for the toast, `inN` for the odds line).

## Crown tiers IV and V (v297)
Every building (all 28 in `ALL_T`) has five crown enhancements in the Armory instead of three. Source: `_backup/crowns_b.js`, inserted by
`_backup/p_crowns.py` (chain: v289 + `p_tundra.py` + `p_rewards.py` + `p_crowns.py` = v297, + `p_mass.py` = v298). Tests: `tests/crown-test.js` (43 checks);
sheets: `tests/crown-shots.js` (`await __cw.sheet(name,types,[E...],{P,size,cols})`), `_backup/shots/crown-1..4.jpg` (tiers III/IV/V),
`crown-parts.jpg` (with upgraded parts), `crown-board.jpg` (on the board at play zoom).
* Names and effects: `CW_ENH`, pushed onto `ENH[k]`. Prices: `enhCost` multipliers `[1,2.2,4.2,6.5,10]`. The Armory checks `ENH[k].length`
  instead of 3; the last two pips are royal (`.arm-pips i.roy`). Saves need nothing new (`meta.enh[k]` simply goes to 4 and 5).
* Stats: `CW_STATS[type](s,E)` from a `structStats` wrapper (range re-capped at 8). Patched in place: mine income and interest (`tStats`),
  windmill and merchant income/aura/bounty (`supportStats`, `merchMul`), the bell, drum and windmill auras (`supportBuff`), the bell aura range (`auraR`).
  New crowd control only raises counts that already exist (targets, hands, roots, pulls), so every stun and push still goes through Resolve.
* Looks: each model ends with `if(E>=4)CRW.<type>(g,head,{its own measurements},E[,spin])` (frost and the ballista inside `towerModel`).
  Tier IV adds a piece of the building itself (drapes, headframe, thunderhead, glass column, bone arch, throne, phoenix wings...);
  tier V adds more and a crown on top: `cwCrown(p,x,y,z,scale,kind)`, one merged vertex-coloured mesh per kind (gold, ghost, blood, void, ice,
  fire = an open ring round the flame, plague) that turns slowly (`onBeforeRender`). Helpers: `cwPennant`, `cwDrape`, `cwShield`, `cwBand` (a gilded ring),
  `cwSeg`, `cwLantern`, `cwTor`. Frost, the ballista and the third batch (drum ... flame) show no looks for tiers I-III; IV and V are their first.

## Materials past gilded marble (v298)
Raising a building's mass goes on changing its material: Runed obsidian at 30, Dragonstone at 40, Celestial crystal at 50 (`MASS_NAMES` has 7 names,
`massX(m)` gives 0..6 while `massTier` still tops out at 3, so every model keeps its tier-3 build). Source `_backup/mass_b.js`, patched in by
`_backup/p_mass.py` after `p_crowns.py`. Tests: `tests/mass-test.js` (16 checks); sheets `__cw.mass(name,types,[20,30,40,50])` in `tests/crown-shots.js`,
`_backup/shots/mass-1..3.jpg`, `mass-board.jpg` (day and night on the board).
* From mass 30 `massCol`/`trimCol` hand out marked colours (`mxCol`, `mxTrim`, registry `MXK`; the obelisk and rune stone bodies, the lantern cage and
  the storm copper go through them too). A `towerModel` wrapper (`mxDress`) swaps those materials for the tier's shader material (`mxMat`,
  program keys `kkmx4..6`: glowing rune glyphs that pulse, molten cracks, gold veins and twinkling stars, all from world position, time in `MXU.uT`),
  gives trims a glowing metal, re-skins the base plate and the light corner quoins, and adds corner accents (obsidian spikes, ember stones,
  floating crystal shards). Wood and iron buildings (catapult, ranger, windmill ...) show it through the plate and accents only. The mine is left as it is.
* The upgrade window and the level-up toast name the new materials (`massX` in `refreshSheet` / `upgradePart`).
* Gotcha: `THREE.Color.getHex()` does not clamp, so a channel above 1 wraps into the next one (a pale blue turned yellow): clamp before `getHex`.

## Milestone bosses (v299)
Waves 100 and 200 of the new lands have their own bosses. Source `_backup/bosses_b.js`, patched in by `_backup/p_bosses.py` after `p_mass.py`.
Tests: `tests/boss-test.js` (31 checks); sheets `tests/boss-shots.js` (`await __bs.sheet(name,types,{size,cols,views})`), `_backup/shots/bosses-2.jpg`, `bosses-board.jpg`.
* Who: `MSB_AT[land][wave]` = [type, total health]; a `bossFor` wrapper returns them (with `milestone:'snow100'` etc.) on the built board
  (`ARENA_BUILT`), so the Plains keep the Pale Elder (100) and the Ophanim (200). Frostvale: the Aurora Drake (a four-winged ice dragon with
  an aurora ribbon, a new dragon kind) and the Winter King (frost giant). Tundra: Irontusk (war-mammoth with a fort on its back) and the Greywind Roc.
  `buildWave` marks the boss with `ms` and never makes it an elite; `snowOk` keeps the Frostvale and tundra re-skins off them; `ENEMY_VARIANTS`,
  `GAL_UNITS`, `EN_NOTE` and `RES_BASE` know them (Bestiary).
* Abilities (`MSB_ACT`, from a `moveEnemy` wrapper): the Drake's breath freezes up to 2 towers near it every 9 s and at half health it gains an
  aurora shield (15% of its health); the Winter King stomps every 8 s (freezes up to 3 towers) and calls 3 knights at 66% and 33%; Irontusk charges
  (`chargeT`) with every Greywind gust and drives nearby foes on, and sheds its iron plates at half health (-0.25 armour); the Roc caps the time to the
  next gust at 12 s and its lightning stuns 2 towers every 10 s. Frozen or stunned towers (`msbFreeze`, a `towerUpdate` wrapper) skip their turn for
  1.5-2.5 s and are immune for 5 s after (`iceImm`), so a board is never locked.
* The milestone: `msbIntro` (title card `#msbCard`, shake, flash, rumble), `msbBarTick` (the top bar `#msbBar`, from the render hook), `msbDefeat`
  (slow motion via `worldSlow`, bursts, shockwaves, fireworks, then the card with the reward; the plain "slain" and Bestiary toasts are held back and
  the new-scroll banner is queued after the card). `msbReward`: the first kill of each pays 150 gems + 150 crowns + a Rare-Legendary scroll (wave 100)
  or 300 gems + 400 crowns + an Epic-Mythic scroll (200), and counts in `meta.msb`; later kills pay 40 / 80 gems. The Roc's death sets `GUST_CALM` = 5
  waves (a `gustTick` wrapper, counted down in `lmWaveClear`, saved as `r.gcalm`). The victory screen names the Winter King and the Roc.

## Bestiary page and softer map borders (v300)
Source `_backup/polish_b.js`, patched in by `_backup/p_polish.py` after `p_bosses.py`. Checks at the end of `tests/boss-test.js` (36 in all).
* The Bestiary lays out `GAL_LIST` from `GAL_UNITS` once at load, so units added later need their own `GAL_LIST` entries (same slot rule): the four
  milestone bosses fill a fifth page; a `buildGallery` wrapper fits their wings to the pedestal (width 2.1); `EN_NOTE` says which land and wave.
* World map: the colour wash takes `wmpBioSoft` (gentler falloff, stronger border warp and a noise term per biome, squared weights, cached on a
  110x154 grid) instead of `wmpBio` (still used for logic: `wmpTop`, sites, mountains). Trees, shrubs and tufts pick their biome with
  `wmpBioPick` (soft weights squared, position hash, so the rest of the map RNG is unchanged), so they mingle near borders. Every keep stays in
  its own land (weight > 0.8). Painting costs about 0.3 s more (once per session). Sheet: `_backup/shots/map-blend-compare.jpg`.

## Abyssal Genesis (ancient), the Aeon Dial (mythic) and the Eldritch Tree (ancient) (v301, v302)
Source `_backup/ancient_core.js` + `ancient_abyss.js` + `ancient_aeon.js` + `ancient_tree.js`, patched in by `_backup/p_ancient.py` after `p_polish.py`
(usage `python _backup/p_ancient.py src/kingsroad-keep.html _backup`). Planned as three ancient domains; in v301 Simon made the first two mythic, in v302
Abyssal Genesis went back to ancient (20 s, cd 170) with the anchor; the Aeon Dial stays mythic (13 s, cd 110); the First Tree was renamed the Eldritch
Tree (key `firsttree` kept for saves; cd 150). Tests: `tests/ancient-test.js` (45 checks); frame sheets
`await __an.sheet(name,k,[t...])` in `tests/ancient-shots.js` (needs `tests/tundra-shots.js` loaded first), `_backup/shots/anc-*.jpg`.
* Shared state `ANC`: `ANC.fs[k]` speed factors (multiplied in `ancF(e)`, which also reads a foe's `ageS`, in the same line as `DOM.f*PURG.f`),
  `ANC.live[k]` (a `refresh()` that winds a running one back, and its time `T`), `ANC.L[k]` the light of each one; the newest (`ANC.top`) is
  blended over the day light in the frame by `ancLight()` (weight `L.k`, sun direction `L.sunOff`), right after the Purgatory light line.
* `ancBegin`/`ancRun` give each one a root group, owned geometries and materials (disposed with the fx; never register cached `box()`/`cyl()`
  geometry), an `SG_ACTIVE` cleanup so `resetCine` takes everything away mid-cast, and `ancPool` (a one-draw-call particle ring buffer).
* The camera is steep (66 degrees), so nothing beyond the board's far end or in the sky is ever seen: each spell places its great pieces inside
  the view at cast time (`ancView`, `ancGround(nx,ny)`), and keeps ruins, kelp and the trunk off roads and towers (`ancFree`, the trunk's spot is
  scored along the top of the view).
* The opening: `ancIntro` shows a rune-ring card. Ancient = `ANCIENT · OLDER THAN TIME`, time almost stops (`worldSlow` .04) and the canvas turns
  sepia for 0.65 s then fades back. Mythic = a `MYTHIC` card only.
* Abyssal Genesis (ice): a black anchor (shank, curved arms with flukes, stock, ring, glowing runes, barnacles, weed) drops on a 64-link chain at 0.55 s,
  planted so its seat (`SEAT_L` on the stock) is right above the hero; crater, glowing cracks, a vortex at its foot; hauled back up at the end. The hero sits
  on it: `ANC.seat` ({H,k,y,yaw,legs}) drives a `heroUpdate` wrapper (lifts `H.g`, forces `sitA`, faces `yaw`, drops move orders) and `heroCasting`
  (patched in `p_ancient.py`, like `CIT.hero`) keeps it casting; legged heroes sit with their hips 0.058 above the origin, robed ones 0.1 lower.
  The sea rises over the field (surface and caustic shaders, light shafts, kelp and shells round the view, monoliths round the hero, vents of bubbles),
  jellyfish (bells + Line tentacles) drift up, three fish schools wheel, the whale shadow (`ShapeGeometry`, renderOrder 63) crosses the view at 6.5-15.5 s,
  two leviathans (24-segment rope chains) lunge along the densest road; drowning every 0.5 s; the backwash at 20 s pushes with `pushE` (Resolve budget).
* The Aeon Dial (light): a colossal clock face of additive strokes (`aeonStrokes`, Roman numerals by `aeonRoman`/`aeonPlace`) with gears, a gold
  bezel and hands, laid over the view; the minute hand turns 4 times (`AEON_ACT` = [3.5, 16.3]) and ages every foe it crosses (`e.ageS`, cleared at
  midnight and on end); a `tStats` wrapper makes towers 20% faster in that window; marble ruins rise round the rim; `L.sunOff` circles so every
  shadow sweeps round. Midnight: hands meet at XII, 1000% to all, non-bosses under 20% crumble.
* The Eldritch Tree (dark): a twisted trunk (displaced cylinder, vertex-coloured moss, sap veins) tears up at the top of the view. Its foot (radius
  `FOOT` 2.3) never touches a road tile, a tower or the hero (hard rule; best spot along the top of the view, then anywhere on the board, then beside
  the board on the low ground); its roots may cross roads. Buttress and field
  roots are tubes with `treeRootMat` (grows along uv.x, tapers, sap pulses); they must not cast shadows (the depth pass ignores the discard). Dappled
  canopy light, shafts and falling leaves. Root eruptions every 0.6 s at the densest unhit knot of foes (`stunE`, once per foe per 4 s). Bloom at 20 s:
  blossoms, a pollen wave (16 tiles/s, every foe hit by 22 s), +3 lives.

## Plain bosses, a health ceiling, the merchant at 1% (v303)
Source `_backup/balance_b.js`, patched in by `_backup/p_balance.py` after `p_ancient.py`. Tests: `tests/balance-test.js` (16 checks).
* v304: at Simon's request this update got brand new patch notes: `p_balance.py` replaces NOTES_HTML and bumps NOTES_V secrets-1 to bosses-1 (shows once for everyone). Later changes go into bosses-1.
* v305 (`_backup/fixes_b.js` + `p_fixes.py`): developer mode opens the whole Bestiary (a `devPin` wrapper fills `meta.dex` for every `GAL_UNITS` variant, so
  units added later open at the next load; `dex` is in `DEV_KEYS`, so switching it off gives the real Bestiary back). Before, only the one-off 'unlock everything'
  button did it. Wing fix in `bosses_b.js` (old copy `bosses_b.v300.js`): the Roc's and the Aurora Drake's left wings were the right ones turned half round
  (rotation.y = PI), which put the feathers and membrane in front of the leading edge; they are now mirrored with `scale.x = -1`. Checks in `tests/boss-test.js`
  (39); sheets `tests/wing-shots.js` (`__ws.sheet`, top and front views), `_backup/shots/wingbefore.jpg` / `wingafter.jpg`.
* v306 (`p_fixes.py`): bosses carry at most one modifier: from wave 100 half the bosses roll one elite affix again (never two, never a milestone boss;
  `spawnEnemy` keeps only the first affix for a boss). The rest of this point still holds. v303 had: bosses never carry a modifier: `buildWave` hands the boss `elite:null`, and `spawnEnemy` skips elite affixes, the gem carrier, the Ill Omen haste/iron and the
  Starfall slow/heal for `d.boss`; the landmark haste (`MK.haste`, marks module) skips bosses too. Scroll Bearer bosses (wave 100+) stay: they change no stats.
* No foe that is not a boss may have more health (plus shield) than `BOSS_CAP_K` (0.5) times the weaker of the two bosses either side of its wave, at its
  wave (`nonBossCap(w)`, `bossHpAt(B,w)` repeats spawnEnemy's boss formula incl. land and tier; cached per land and wave). A `spawnEnemy` wrapper scales
  `maxHp`, `hp` and the shield down and marks `en.capped`. Before v303 a giant two-affix ram had up to 1.13 times a boss at waves 25-39.
* Testing in a hidden Browser pane: the window is 0x0 and the camera aspect NaN; give the tab a viewport (`resize_window` 430x860) or tap/layout checks fail.
  `ancView` falls back to the camera target framing when the camera gives no view.

## Land places (v299)
Frostvale and the tundra use none of the Plains landmarks or chests. Source `_backup/marks_b.js`, appended to the tundra module by `p_tundra.py`.
Tests: `tests/marks-test.js` (36 checks); sheets `__tun.marks(land)` in `tests/tundra-shots.js`, `_backup/shots/marks-snow.jpg`, `marks-tundra.jpg`.
* `MK_SETS`: Frostvale `lodge, falls, wmother, den, caravan, huts`; tundra `wreck, stones, herders, lighthouse, seer, ivory`. `placeLandmarks` draws
  from `lmKeys()`, which hands out the land's set on boards of version 3 (`MAP_GV`, saved as `r.gv`): runs saved before keep their old places.
* Each place is an `LMD` entry with its own model (`markModel`, through an `lmModel` wrapper on the usual clearing), choices (`lmOptions` wrapper),
  states (`lmSetState`, `LM_DONE`), map icon (`lmIcon`), animation (`lmAnim`) and effects: trapper slow aura (lodge), ice-well speed and lighthouse
  range (`tStats` wrapper), wolf damage near the den, harpoons (+40% to bosses, `t.harp` in a `hurt` wrapper), the Winter Mother's slow or haste,
  calm (`GUST_CALM`) or call the wind (+50% gold, gusts every 15 s), tributes from the huts and the herders, lives, gems, scraps. Counters live in `MK`
  (saved as `r.mk`). Chests: a `chestModel` wrapper recolours them and adds an ice shell (Frostvale, it breaks on opening) or rope handles and
  barnacles (tundra). Cut trees are named Snow-laden Pine / Wind-bent Spruce.

Rebuild from scratch: `sh _backup/rebuild.sh` (v289 + every patch in order, then `python build.py`); the result must equal the newest `_backup/kingsroad-keep.vNNN.html`. Add each new patch as a line at the end. Tests: run every `tests/*-test.js` (other sessions add suites), on a page loaded after the rebuild finished.

## Changelog (v307)
Source: `_backup/changelog_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_changelog.py` on top of v306. `CLOG` lists every update since the world map, newest first
({t, d, v, li}); `showChangelog()` opens it from More > Patch notes (`#bChangelog`) and the What's new popup (`.np-log`). Add each new update at the top of CLOG.

## Terrain: high ground, marsh and ruins (v308)
Source: `_backup/terrain_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_terrain.py` on top of v307. Tests: `tests/terrain-test.js` (38 checks).
Rebuild chain: ... + `p_changelog.py` (= v307) + `p_terrain.py` (= v308, `_backup/kingsroad-keep.v308.html`).
* Generation (`terGen`, called inside `buildMap` right after `placeLore`, own RNG `seed ^ 0x6b43a9b5`, so trees, rocks, chests, landmarks and lore are
  unchanged): 2-4 high-ground spots on road-side build spots (Manhattan 5 apart), 2-4 ruins on road-side spots (grid 2; never touching each other, a
  hill or the first 6 road tiles of a gate; `terFair` keeps 12 free spots, or what the gate had, within 2.7 of each gate's first 6 road tiles and lets
  no 8-tile stretch of road lose more than 2 road-side spots), 1-3 marsh patches of 3-5 road tiles (not the first 6 or the last 4 of a route, 3 apart).
  `TER.k` holds the kind per cell (1 high, 2 marsh, 3 ruins). Hills are held as grid 2 while the land dressers run (no snow drift or tuft lands on
  them) and released by the outer `buildMap` wrapper (`terRelease`), which then calls `terDress`.
* Effects: `tStats`/`heroStats` wrappers multiply range by 1.15 on high ground (after the cap, so it can pass 7.5). `gy` and `buildTowerMesh` lift
  whatever stands on a hill by `TER.lift` (.28). Marsh and the slow cap live in `terSlow(e, ordinary, legendary)`, called from `moveEnemy`: the
  ordinary slows (e.slow from buildings and spells, the Frost spell's Blizzard) times the marsh (troops .75, bosses and Unstoppable foes .875,
  flyers 1) go through `landSlow` and never below `TER_FLOOR` (.25); Glacial Rift's blizzard, domains, chrono and citadel multiply on top.
  With no marsh and no cap hit it is exactly the old `landSlow(o*b)`. Marsh does not touch the Resolve budget.
* Looks (`terDress`, `TERP` palettes per land): a plateau with a craggy cliff per hill (icicles in Frostvale, spikes in the tundra), mud rim + bog
  water + reeds + lily pads / ice plates / peat per marsh tile, floor slabs + an L of broken wall + a column + a drum + rubble per ruin. All built
  from a few cached shapes (`terShape`, `TER_SH`) transformed straight into 3 merged meshes (`terGB.add` takes a triangle list and a Matrix4):
  about 10 ms a board. Ripples (`terRipple`, a pool of 32 rings) where foes wade, driven by an `update` wrapper (`terTick`).
* UI: tapping a terrain tile shows `#terTip` (`terTap`, from the end of `tap`); the range stat gets a gold triangle (`terRangeTag` in `statLabel`);
  the range ring gets a gold band for the bonus and a line at the base range (`terBand`, `terRingSync` via `refreshSheet`/`movePlacing` wrappers);
  the placement tip says "+15% range" on a hill and "nothing can be built here" on ruins; the home preview draws them (`terPreview`).
* Saves: `serializeRun` stores `ter:{h,m,u}` (cell indices); `loadRun` replays them through `TER_LOAD` (checked against the grid). A run without
  `ter` (saved before v308) gets no terrain. Developer panel: Terrain on/off (`terDevToggle`, `meta.terOff`, only honoured while developer mode is on).

## Finer materials (v309)
Source: `_backup/matfix_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_matfix.py` on top of v308 (after the v298 mass module). Runed obsidian (mass 30), Dragonstone
(40) and Celestial crystal (50) no longer use the full-surface shader patterns: `mxMat` now returns flat-shaded Phong stone and `MX_TRIMMAT` a
softly glowing trim, with new colours in `MX_BASE` / `MX_TRIM` / `MX_PLATE` / `MX_QUOIN` (indigo with silver-blue, oxblood with ember-bronze,
moonstone blue with gold). `mxCol`, `mxTrim`, `mxDress` and the corner accents are unchanged. Only looks change.

## Hero Archive sort (v310)
Source: `_backup/hsort_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_hsort.py` on top of v309. The `#archSort` select became `#archSortBtn`, which opens
`#hsortPop` (a card menu built by `hsortOpen`; closes on outside tap, Esc or a pick). Orders: rarity (stars, then price, best first), `rarityAsc`
(worst first, new), level, power, recent. Rarity used to sort by price alone, which put 2-star Old Tobin among the 1-star heroes; it is the star
count first now. The choice is saved in `meta.archSort`. `hsortInit()` runs from the module itself (the archive's wiring IIFE runs before it).

## Developer speed (v311)
`_backup/p_devspeed.py` (no module): `speedCap` returns 5 while developer mode is on, so the speed button goes 1-2-3-4-5x on any wave (otherwise 2.5x on waves not beaten yet). The developer panel footer says so. Checks in `tests/dev-test.js` (48).

## Marsh on the Kingsroad only, blended (v312)
`_backup/marsh_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_marsh.py` on top of v311. Wrappers on `terRoll` / `terApply` drop marsh outside arena 1 (also when a saved run carries marsh cells); a `terDress` wrapper draws hills and ruins with the old code and the marsh with `terMarshDress`. Marsh tiles that touch are one bog (`terMarshGroups`, 4-connected, whichever patch they came from). Each bog is a soft field (a pool per tile, a smaller one beside it, one between every two touching tiles, a little value noise) traced with marching squares into two smooth layers just above the road: wet ground whose rim is the exact colour of the road tile under it (`TILES.getColorAt`), and water that darkens toward the middle (polygonOffset, no z-fighting). Reeds and lily pads follow the shore, tufts soften the grass edge. About 7 ms a board. Tests: `tests/terrain-test.js` (41).

## Thick snow in Frostvale (v313)
`_backup/snowdrift_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_snowdrift.py` on top of v312. `terMarshLand` now lets the marsh stretches be generated in the Plains and Frostvale (not the tundra). In Frostvale they are thick snow: `terMarshF` gives 15% (bosses and Unstoppable foes 7.5%), divided by the land's stronger-slow factor so the drift alone is exactly 15% (it is not a spell or a building); `terTipText` says "Thick snow"; `terMarshDress` hands over to `terSnowDress`, the same soft field (`terMarshGroups`) traced into one raised, smooth-shaded snow surface whose rim is the road tile's colour; `terRipple` kicks up snow puffs (`sprayP`) instead of rings. Tests: `tests/terrain-test.js` (46).

## Tidier boards (v314)
`_backup/tidy_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_tidy.py` on top of v313. Board version `MAP_GV` 4: `genMap` calls `tidyWoods(R)` instead of the old forest + sprinkle (3-5 groves on the most open ground, edges welcome, at least 5 tiles apart; 1-3 rock outcrops of 2-4 stones away from the groves), and nothing grows within one tile of a road (`tidyNearRoad`). Story props drop from 4-7 to 3-4, and Frostvale draws about 10 drifts, only against a grove or the board edge (`tidyDriftOk`). Saved runs rebuild with their own `r.gv`, so older runs keep their old board; `loadRun` puts `MAP_GV` back to 4. Tests: `tests/tidy-test.js` (18).

## Natural groves (v315)
`_backup/treejit_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_treejit.py` on top of v314. Trees and board stones get a seeded offset inside their tile (`jitHash` of the seed and tile, never the board RNG, so every layout and saved run is unchanged): `treeJit()` spaces neighbours apart (canopy radius about .32 x size), makes grove hearts taller, and gives about a third of the inner trees a sapling, a child of its tree (`treeJitKids`) so cutting, burning and bending take it along. Stones (`rockJit`) are tagged `userData.rk` with their tile; `rockIndex` finds them by that tag now. Tests: `tests/tidy-test.js` (24). `tests/run-all.js` runs every suite in one go (`__runAll()`, results on `window.__R`).

## Scrolls batch 6 (v316)
Ten scrolls, two per rarity, six of them fire. Data goes in before `SCROLL_IDS` and the code (`_backup/spells6_b.js`) goes in before the scroll art is built; both are added by `_backup/p_spells6.py` on top of v315. Ids: flamelash, thunderclap (common), catchflame, firewall (uncommon), firetornado, frostwolves (rare), dragonbreath, prism (epic), volcano, tempestspire (legendary). Shared pieces: `s6FlameMat` is a noise flame shader. It is normally blended by default so it keeps its colour on snow; `add` makes a glow layer, `k` makes smoke, and surfaces fade where they turn edge-on. `S6FZ` lists the burning ground (cinders, lava pools, fire trails, walls of fire) for Firestorm. `s6Spot` finds free ground for the volcano and spire, away from towers and the hero. Synergies: Wildfire (Catch Flame leaps to every Charged foe), Firestorm (the tornado drinks burning ground, x1.25 size and +50% damage), and Lightning Rod (the spire hits foes charged by something else +50%; its own charge, `e.s6sp`, does not count). The dragon re-aims at the densest foes every 0.12s, because a straight cone down the road cut across bends. Walk-test value per second at P100 sits inside each rarity band (common 33, uncommon 35-38, rare 46-53, epic ~57, legendary 44-46). Tests: `tests/scrolls6-test.js` (13 rows) and three new combos in `tests/spells-test.js`. `_backup/s6_swap.py` swaps whole functions in a module from a blocks file.

## Scroll changes (v317)
`_backup/p_scrolls7.py` + `_backup/scrolls7_b.js` (blocks `//@@CATCH` and `//@@PRISM`), applied on top of v316. Simon withdrew Dragon's Breath, Volcanic Eruption and Tempest Spire. Their code, data, hooks and the Lightning Rod synergy are cut from v316 by marker regions; `s6Spot` went with them. `s6Withdraw` (run on the loaded account and wrapped round `migrateMeta`) deletes any of the three from a save and refunds its scraps (rarity scrap plus the upgrades spent: up x lvl x (lvl-1)/2), with a one-time toast. Catch Flame was reworked: a seed of fire flies on a ribbon trail (`s6Leap` + `nsTrail`), each ignition gets a crisp ring, and burning foes carry real flame tongues (`s6Engulf`: tuft meshes sharing the cast's flame materials). Prism of Dawn was reworked too: shards gather, the faces are rainbow (`s6PrismGeo`), the beams get shafts and hot spots and leave a colour trail, and it folds into a lance with a six-ring rainbow nova (`s6PrismLance`, 180% in radius 1.8). Walk value: Catch Flame 38.5, Prism 59 (epic band 57-61). Tests: `tests/scrolls6-test.js`.

## Artifacts (v318)
`_backup/artifacts_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_artifacts.py` before the boot line, on top of v317. `ARTS` holds 30 artifacts: one per element (fire, ice, lightning, light, dark) at each rarity from common to mythic, with effect text `d`, lore `l` and an SVG glyph in `ART_G`; `artIcon(id,found)` draws the medallion, whose frame grows with rarity. Save: `meta.arts={own,eq:{hero:[3 ids]},pend:[{w,o:[ids]}]}`; `artFix` repairs it (wrapped round `migrateMeta`), and `DEV_KEYS.arts` keeps developer mode honest. Sockets: `artSlots()` = 1 + `wonA.snow` + `wonA.tundra` (3 in developer mode). In play: `artActive()` is the battle hero's artifacts in open sockets, and effects follow the element of the hit (any source). Hooks wrap `hurt` (multipliers and on-hit), `kill` (on-kill, via `e.lastO`), `applyBurn`, `statusTick`, `castScrollNow` (Tempest Heart, Sun Disc), `comboTick` (Bell Tower reveal, Glacier Rune ground) and `resetCine`; artifact damage carries `o.art` so it never triggers itself. Its scale is `artP()`, the hero's spell power. Invisible: `AFFIX.invisible`; a `spawnEnemy` wrapper keeps it to wave 30+, never on bosses, and rarer than the other modifiers. It applies ghost materials (`artGhostMat`, `invisLook`), and `inRange` hides these foes from crewed towers (TD kind phys, siege or pierce) and from non-mage heroes until `e.revealT`. Weakened: `e.weakT` gives +15% damage taken and 15% slow. Drops: `lmWaveClear` on every 100th wave calls `artDrop` -> `artRoll` (3 unowned, rarity from `ART_DROP`, falling back to the nearest rarity still missing; gems once everything is owned) and `artOfferShow` (pauses, pick 1 of 3, auto-places into a free socket of the battle hero; waits behind the victory screen). UI: the `#archTabs` Heroes / Artifacts switch, `renderArts` (the hero strip, three rune sockets, the collection with hidden powers) and `artModal`; `renderHeroDetail` adds a socket row. Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (53).

## Artifact fix (v319)
`_backup/p_artfix.py`, on top of v318. Cinder Heart and Shatter Shard blast foes when one dies; the blast could reach the dying foe before it was marked dead, so it was killed again and again (627 kills from 12 foes in the check; about 100x gold). The `kill` wrapper now marks `e.artK` and kills each foe once. Test row: 'a chain of blasts kills each foe only once (v319)'.

## The artifact chamber (v320)
`_backup/artui_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_artui.py` before the boot line, on top of v319. Simon's sketch: `renderArts` is replaced by a stone chamber (`.avc`): a brick wall, a breathing shaft of light with rays and motes, grooves joining the three sockets laid out in a triangle (`ART_SPOS`), and a pedestal (`ART_PED`). `artSockSvg(state,id)` draws each socket: grey carved stone; empty shows a faint glowing rune, full shows the rarity ring and the artifact, locked is crossed by two interlocking chains with a brass-keyhole padlock. An `artClick` wrapper answers a tap on a locked socket with a note inside the chamber ('Win wave 200 in Frostvale / the Greywind Tundra to open this socket'), kept between the walls, and a shake. `#artView` is the scroll container (`#artView>*{flex-shrink:0}` keeps the hero strip from collapsing). Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (56).

## The artifact hall (v321)
`_backup/artroom_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_artroom.py` before the boot line, on top of v320. Simon found the v320 picture flat ('like Geometry Dash'), the pedestal a template, the wall too close and the chains not holding anything. The chamber is now a real 3D room on its own small WebGL renderer (`artRoom()`, like the hero preview): instanced flagstones and a nave of pillars into fog, a far wall with a dim pointed archway, torches (two point lights) and candles, the round stepped altar of the double dungeon (block-seam texture on the risers, carved top with faintly glowing rune rings), a spot light from above with a soft shaft and dust, and the three sockets floating above the altar (`AR_POS`, scale `AR_SC`). Empty sockets glow with a rune, full ones show the artifact (its `artIcon` SVG drawn to a texture) with a rarity ring and halo, and locked ones are bound by two chain bands wrapped round the stone (`arBand`, links via `arLinks` on one InstancedMesh) staked to iron eyes on the altar (`AR_ANC`), with a padlock where the bands cross. `arSync` rebuilds a socket when its state changes; `arFrame` (run from the `renderPreview` hook) animates and renders, and `arPlace` keeps the v320 socket buttons and labels over the drawn sockets. Tapping a locked socket rattles it; the v320 note now sits under the tapped socket. Without WebGL the v320 picture stays. About 2.6 ms a frame, 121 draw calls. `_backup/ar_shot.js` saves the room to the shot server. Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (62).

## Artifact balance (v322)
`_backup/artbal_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_artbal.py` before the boot line, on top of v321 (the patcher also edits the v318 artifact code in place). The five changes from the artifact review, approved by Simon: (1) Conductor's Ring leaps on 35% of lightning hits for 35% of the hit. (2) Ice: Rime Band +15%; Glacier Rune's ground chills and deals 8% of hero power every 0.5 s (one patch at a time, `comboTick`); Winter's Grip bursts 25% of an ice hit on a chilled foe into up to 3 foes around (`artGrip`); Winter King +15% ice damage and its statue shatters for 300% hero power (`artStatueBurst`). (3) Attunement: `artEl(o)` gives the battle hero's plain attacks (no `o.el`, not spells or artifact hits) the element of the first filled socket (`artAttune`); attuned ice hits chill, attuned fire hits singe (`artAttFx`, burn 3% of the hit for 2 s); the kill effects read `e.lastEl`; the Artifacts tab says what a hero is attuned to. (4) Invisible: a `buildWave` wrapper applies the Invisible rules when the wave is built and marks kept ones `invOK` (the spawn wrapper keeps them), `waveSummary` adds 'N invisible' to the call button, `callWave` warns (`artInvWarn`, a full explanation the first time via `meta.invSeen`), and the Bestiary cycles to an Invisible ghost (`ELITE_CYCLE`, `galElite`). (5) Weakened from Hexed Nail (20% of dark hits, 3 s) and Plague Mark (the foe its wither leaps to, 4 s). Benchmark: `_backup/art_bench.js` (`__abRun(jobs,reps)`, paired fights on board seed 31; it runs `animHero` so the hero's attacks fly). Results v321 -> v322: Conductor's Ring +64% -> +18%; Glacier Rune +3% -> +25%; Winter's Grip ~0 -> +22%; Winter King ~0 -> +20% (+9 kills); Hexed Nail +1% -> +14%; Robin (hero only) with a Common 0 -> ~+10%, with Conductor's Ring 0 -> +16%. Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (78).

## Milestone rewards (v323)
`_backup/p_msbrew.py`, on top of v322. Simon halved the one-time rewards of the milestone bosses and took the scroll away: `msbReward` now pays 75 gems and 75 crowns for the first wave-100 kill (Aurora Drake, Irontusk) and 150 gems and 200 crowns for the first wave-200 kill (Winter King, Greywind Roc), plus the trophy. Repeat kills still pay 40 / 80 gems. Tests: `tests/boss-test.js`.

## One artifact per drop (v324)
`_backup/artone_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_artone.py` before the boot line, on top of v323. Simon found the pick of three too strong. `artRoll(w)` now rolls the rarity on `ART_DROP` and returns one of that rarity's five artifacts at random (owned or not). `artGrant(w,id)` grants it at once (into a free open socket of the equipped hero) or, for a duplicate, adds `ART_SCRAP` (1/2/5/10/20/40 by rarity) to `meta.arts.scrap` (no use yet; repaired by an `artFix` wrapper, shown as 'N SCRAP' next to the Artifacts count). `artDrop` queues the granted result in `meta.arts.pend` ({w,id,dup,sc,placed,g:1}); `artOfferShow` shows one reveal card (rays that grow with rarity, 'Collect' / 'Take the scraps'). A pick-of-three offer saved before v324 becomes one of its three at random. The gems-when-complete fallback is gone (duplicates give scraps). Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (81).

## Artifacts power spells (v325)
`_backup/p_artspell.py`, on top of v324. Simon: artifacts should affect the spells the hero uses, not its main damage. `artEl(o)` now returns an element only for hits from the hero's scrolls (`o.spell`, not artifact hits `o.art`); the hero's own attacks, towers and the artifacts' blasts no longer set artifacts off. v322's attunement (`artAttune`, `artAttFx`, the attuned line in the Artifacts tab) is removed. Kill effects need the killing blow from a spell (`e.lastEl`; a spell's burn carries the spell), so artifact blasts no longer chain into more blasts. Rime Band's bonus applies to spell hits only. Statuses an artifact puts on a foe (Weakened, the Hexed Nail's armour strip) still count for every hit. The Artifacts tab reads 'they power this hero's spells'. Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (79).

## Arena 4: the Whitewaste (v326)
`_backup/ww_b.js` + `wwboss_b.js` + `wwmarks_b.js`, inserted together by `_backup/p_whitewaste.py` before the boot line, on top of v325. The map site `whitewaste` (biome snowtundra) becomes a playable land: `ARENAS.whitewaste` (no 4), `SITE_ARENA`, `arenaNow`; it opens with a wave-200 win in Frostvale or the tundra (`arenaWin` wrapper, plus an `arenaFix` wrapper for accounts that already won); the trip from the Plains follows the roads (`wmpVia`). Board (`whitewasteBoard`): snow tiles 0xd8e0e8, a dark glacier-ice road 0x3c4a5c, `wwGround` shader (wind ridges and glitter on the snow, light crack lines on the road, tile sides lifted so the seams stay faint), frost-killed spruce via `WW_REMAP` + `snowTopMat`, and instanced decoration outside the grid tagged `userData.ww` (edge ice shards, sastrugi ridges, ice spires, dead trees, frozen-city pillars, blue ice, an ice wall far behind; nothing tall on the near side). `WW_LIGHT`, `WW_PV`, `TERP.whitewaste`, `LAND_EL.whitewaste` (ice 1.15, slow 1.15), `ARENA_REWARD.whitewaste` (gems x1.75, rarity +12%), up to three gates (`gateN`), Frostvale's falling snow plus blowing snow over the border (`wwAtmos`). Foes: troops x1.25 HP, bosses x1.4 (`spawnEnemy`, `bossHpAt`), rime-pale look `wwify`/`wwMat` with ice shards `wwGear`, frozen breath. The rising dead (`WWR`, `riseTick`/`riseStart`/`riseAt`): every 35-50 s while a wave is on the field, 1.8 s of warning (glowing cracks `riseFxOn`, `#wwPill`, `sfxRise`), then 3 + wave/40 (max 8) frozen dead rise mid-road (70% HP, half gold) over ~0.9 s standing in place (`emT`, the group sinks). `wwCalm(n)` keeps them down for n waves (saved as `r.wwr`). Developer menu row 'Rising dead'. Bosses (`wwboss_b.js`): Hollowmaw (100; world segments `hmSegs`/`hmSegTick`, dives every 9 s, 6 s when wounded: `e.burrow` hides it from `inRange`, then bursts out and freezes 2 towers) and the Rimebound Lich (200; raises dead every 11 s, frost nova every 13 s on 3 towers, phylactery shield + 6 dead at half HP; its death calms the dead for 3 waves). Both in the Bestiary (the last two slots of page 4). Places (`wwmarks_b.js`): props icebound, giantskull, wbanner, watchfire, obelisk, crevasse (`wwPropModel` via a `bioModel` wrapper); landmarks beacon (no rising within 4 tiles, towers +10% rate), tomb (loot: gold + gems, dead twice as often for 3 waves; seal: calm 3 waves), outpost (ballista shoots within 3.5 tiles), pilgrims (+3 lives), icecave (gold + scrap, or lenses +12% range), colossus (slow aura, or take the crown: +5 gems, foes faster 3 waves); an ice-bound chest. Dev helpers: `_backup/ww_shot.js`. Tests: `tests/whitewaste-test.js` (44).

## Crafting and the Kingsroad's milestones (v327)
`_backup/artcraft_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_craft.py` before the boot line, on top of v326. Simon's numbers: duplicates give `ART_SCRAP` 1/2/4/8/15/35 scraps (common to mythic; was 1/2/5/10/20/40), and `artCraft(id)` crafts an artifact you do not have for `ART_CRAFT` 10/30/60/115/250/600 scraps: it goes through `artGrant` (free socket of the equipped hero) and the reveal card ('Crafted from artifact scraps'). The card of a missing artifact (`artModal` wrapper) offers 'Craft' with the cost; missing cards in the collection show their cost (`.av-craft`, gold when affordable). The Kingsroad's wave 100 and 200 bosses (the Pale Elder, the Ophanim) pay a first-kill reward like the other lands (`plainsBossReward`, a `kill` wrapper on bosses with `e.wv` 100/200 in the Plains): 75 gems + 75 crowns, 150 gems + 200 crowns, counted in `meta.msb.plains100/200`, shown on a milestone card; later kills pay nothing extra. Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (86), `tests/boss-test.js` (43).

## Artifact levels (v328)
`_backup/artlv_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_artlv.py` before the boot line, on top of v327 (the patch also swaps every fixed artifact number in the hurt wrapper, `artOnHit`, `artOnKill` and the helpers for `artV(id)`). Every artifact goes from level 1 (exactly as it was) to 5, bought with artifact scraps: `ART_UP` costs to level 2/3/4/5 are Common 3/5/8/10, Uncommon 10/15/25/30, Rare 20/30/45/60, Epic 35/60/85/115, Legendary 75/125/190/250, Mythic 180/300/450/600 (the last step equals the crafting price). `ART_LVD[id]` holds each artifact's five values `v` and the text they make `d(v)`; `artLvDesc()` rewrites `ART_BY[id].d` for the current levels. Commons gain damage (+10/13/16/20/25%); the others grow one thing the player can feel: Thunder King bolts 2..6 (Simon's example), Conductor's Ring 1..3 leaps (`artConduct`), Phoenix Ash 1..3 embers, Plague Mark 1..3 targets, Shatter Shard 3..8 shards, Tempest Heart every 5th..3rd cast and up to +40%, Seraph Halo cooldown 2..1 s, Grave Pact x2..x3 gold, and so on. Saved as `meta.arts.lv` (clamped 1-5 by the `artFix` wrapper). The artifact card shows level pips, what the next level brings (the words that change lit by `artLvDiff`) and 'Upgrade for N scraps' (`[data-up]`, through `artClick`); the collection shows 'Lv N'. Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (99).

## The merchant's black market (v329)
`_backup/blackmarket_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_blackmarket.py` before the boot line, on top of v328. When the wandering merchant arrives (`wmArrive` wrapper) there is a `WM_BM_CH` 25% chance he also opens a black market: `wmBmStock(wave)` rolls two different artifacts, the rarity on `ART_DROP` (`wmBmRow`: the row of the next 100th wave, so the wave 100 table before wave 100, the wave 200 table after it, and so on), then one of that rarity's five, one you do not have before one you do. Prices `WM_BM_PRICE` (Simon): Common 1000, Uncommon 2000, Rare 4000, Epic 8000, Legendary 15000, Mythic 30000 gems. The shop (`wmOpen`) shows a Black Market section under the scrolls (`wmBmHtml`, `[data-bm]`, two taps like the scrolls); `wmBmBuy` goes through `artGrant` (free socket, or scraps for one you have) and the reveal card ('Bought at the black market'). A dark crate under a purple cloth with a purple lantern and a floating sigil (`WM.bmProp`, added by a `wmBuild` wrapper as stall pop 7) marks it. He stays until the scrolls and the black market are both sold out (`wmSoldOut`). Saved as `serializeRun().wm.bm = [[id, price, sold]]`. `WM.bmForce` true/false overrides the roll once (developer panel 'With black market', tests). Tests: `tests/merchant-test.js` (51).

## Fewer gems from waves (v330)
`_backup/p_gems.py` (no module), on top of v329. Simon: 'lower overall gem drop rates from all waves'. `waveGems(w)` is the old formula times `GEM_WAVE_K` .5, rounded, at least 1 (1 / 4 / 17 / 32 gems at waves 1 / 10 / 100 / 200; 1350 over 200 waves instead of 2600, before the land multiplier `arenaGems`). Not changed: gem carriers (2% of non-boss foes; v330 briefly made them 1%, Simon wanted them kept, so the patch was redone and shipped as v331), milestone boss rewards (v323), Bestiary discoveries, chests and landmarks. Tests: `tests/rewards-test.js`.

## Enemy gates per land (v332)
`_backup/p_gates.py` (no module), on top of v331. Simon: Frostvale only 2 spawn points, the tundra 2-3, the Whitewaste only 3. Board version `MAP_GV` 5 (the `var` and the reset in `loadRun`); saved runs rebuild with their own `r.gv`. `gateN(x,seed)` at version 5: snow 2, whitewaste 3, tundra 2 or 3 rolled once per board from its seed (`mulberry(seed^0x2c1b3c6d)`), so a layout that cannot fit three gates is retried with the same count instead of drifting to two; the Plains keep the old roll (1 or 2) and their boards are unchanged. From version 5 `genMap` tries up to 240 gate spots and retries the layout when it places fewer gates than asked. Tests: `tests/arena-test.js` (gate counts per land, Plains unchanged), `tidy-test.js` (follows the current version), `whitewaste-test.js`.

## One gate on the Kingsroad (v333)
`_backup/p_gate1.py`, on top of v332. Simon: the first land opens only 1 enemy gate. Board version `MAP_GV` 6: `gateN` returns 1 for the Plains (it was 1 or 2); runs saved on version 5 or older rebuild their old board (arena-test checks a two-gate version 5 run). Gates now: Kingsroad 1, Frostvale 2, Tundra 2-3, Whitewaste 3.

## Tower damage scaling (v334)
`_backup/p_dmgscale.py`, on top of v333. Simon: 'lower all damage scaling by 1,5% please on towers'. `massMul(n)` (the Damage / mass upgrade of every tower) takes `MASS_CUT` .015 off each stage's per-level factor: 1.265 for levels 1-10, 1.237 for 11-18, 1.183 for 19+ (was 1.28 / 1.252 / 1.198). Compounded: about -11% at Damage 10, -21% at 20, -31% at 30, -46% at 50. Specials (cannon +3%, frost x1.05 per special level) and enhancement tiers are unchanged; the Obelisk follows massMul^0.8. Tests: `tests/balance-test.js` (20).

## Dawn Feather mends the keep (v335)
`_backup/dawn_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_dawn.py` before the boot line, on top of v334. Heroes never take damage (`heroU.hpf` only ever starts at 1), so the old hero heal did nothing. `ART_LVD.dawnfeather.v` is now `[chance, mends a wave, max lives]`: 4/5/6/7/8%, once a wave (twice at level 5), +5/10/15/20/25. `keepMax()` = 20 (+3 per walls, switched off) + `artDawnMax()` (the bonus when the equipped hero has it socketed); `newGame` starts the run at `keepMax()` (Simon: 45 lives at level 5). `artDawn(p)` on a light spell kill (`artOnKill`) gives 1 life back with sparks to the keep, per-wave count in `DAWN`, never above `keepMax()`. Other life gains (landmarks, developer) are not capped. Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (109).

## The merchant stays 2 minutes (v336)
`_backup/wmtime_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_wmtime.py` before the boot line, on top of v335. Simon: on a sped-up game the merchant left before he had set up. His stay was `WM_STAY` (3) cleared waves counted in `wmWave`, so fast waves ran out while he drove in and `wmLeave` fired the moment his stall opened. Now `wmWave` only rolls arrivals; `wmClock(dt)` (called from `wmAnim`'s open state, which gets the frame's unscaled `rdt`: game speed never touches it and paused time is 0) counts `WM.left` down from `WM_STAY_S` 120, warns at 30 s, and calls `wmLeave` at 0 once his shop is closed. Selling out still sends him off early. The shop footer and the developer panel show the time left (`wmFmtT`); `WM.left` is saved with the run (`wm.left`). `wmArrive` refuses while `WM.on`, so there is never a second merchant. Tests: `tests/merchant-test.js` (58).

## Blood storm (v337)
`_backup/bloodrain_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_bloodrain.py` before the boot line, on top of v336. Simon did not like the moon in the top right and wanted the Blood Moon to feel like a storm raining blood everywhere. CSS hides `.bm-moon`, `.bm-ecl` and `.bm-rays`; `bmStormParts()` adds two drifting `.bm-cloud` layers along the top of `#bmSky` (they flash `.lit` with every `bm2Bolt` and with sheet lightning every 2.5-6.5 s) and the `#bmRain` canvas: `bmRainDraw(dt,k)` strokes 160-460 slanted drops in two layers (far thin and dim, near long and bright) on real time, frozen while paused. On the board: splashes (`sprayP`, 30/s), the old falling ash turned into falling blood, embers cut to a quarter, lightning every 2.2-5.4 s (was 4.5-9.5 s), and raindrop ripples in the `bmGround` shader (`rn`). `bmRainSnd(k)` loops band-passed noise into `SFX.master` while it lasts. Everything follows `BM.k`, so it fades in and out with the event. Tests: `tests/dev-test.js` (51).

## Ice Tomb takes the strongest (v338)
`_backup/p_icetomb.py`, on top of v337. Simon: Ice Tomb should always target the strongest unit, whatever the hero's targeting. It already ignored the targeting order but compared current health among foes in the hero's range only, so a wounded knight or a boss further up the road lost to a fresh peasant. `iceTombPick(t,list)` looks at every foe the hero could hit anywhere on the field (`inRange` with range 1e4, so Invisible / burrowed / air rules still apply) and takes a boss first, then the highest `maxHp`, then the most `hp+shield`; a `castIceTomb` wrapper hands it that one foe. Texts updated. Tests: `tests/balance-test.js` (24).

## Building masteries (v339)
`_backup/mastery_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_mastery.py` before the boot line, on top of v338. Simon: the more you use a building, the more upgrades it unlocks, Mastery 5 the max, slow to earn; one building at a time, the Archer Tower first. Account XP per building type in `meta.mast` (repaired by a `migrateMeta` wrapper; `DEV_KEYS.mast` keeps the real account's book in developer mode): a `kill` wrapper gives the killing tower's type 1 XP, 3 for an elite, 30 for a boss (hero kills give none); support buildings (`SUPPORT_T`) get `MAST_SUP` 4 XP per cleared wave each (`lmWaveClear` wrapper). `MAST_XP` 1000/4000/12000/30000/70000 total for Mastery 1-5 (a bot run to wave 30 with 7 of 11 towers archers earned 529, so an archer-heavy run to wave 200 is about 8,000: Mastery 5 in about nine long runs). `MAST_D[type]` lists the five upgrades; the archer's are read in a `tStats` wrapper and used in the archer branch of `towerUpdate`: Keen Eyes (+10% crit, 2.5x), Volley (`archVolley`: every 8th shot multi+3), Piercing Shafts (`archPierce`: the nearest other foe within 1.1 of the impact takes 40%), Hunter's Mark (`archMark`: every 10 s the first target is Weakened 4 s), Rain of Arrows (`archRain`: every 14 s on a crowd of 3+ or a boss, 16 arrows from the sky around `densest`, 45% to foes within 0.55 of each landing, a target ring, arrows left standing in the ground `archStuck`). Level-ups toast and refresh the pennant (`mastFlag`: bronze, silver, gold, sapphire, crimson with a glow, on every tower of that type). UI: `mastRow` in each Armory item (diamonds, bar, next upgrade) opens `mastOpen(k)`; `mastSheet(t)` in the tower sheet (`mastSig` in its signature); developer panel 'Building masteries' Max all / Reset (`mst5`/`mst0`). Tests: `tests/mastery-test.js` (31).

## Aeon Dial and Invisible foes (v340)
`_backup/p_aeon2.py` then `_backup/p_invis.py`, on top of v339. Aeon Dial: each sweep `hurt(e,PW*3.5,...)` (was 1.2) plus the old 1% max HP; the cast cards (`#ancCard`, the ancient/mythic intro, was z-index 31, and the `#cine` spell banners, 7) now sit at z-index 4, under `#bottom` (5), so they no longer cover the tower sheet. Invisible foes: kept only after wave 20 (`<=20` replaced, both in the `buildWave` roll and the `spawnEnemy` guard); `artInvWarn` announces once a run (`INVW.run`, reset by `newGame` and `loadRun` wrappers: 'Invisible foes are starting to appear' plus how to see them); the every-wave toast and the preview's 'N invisible' are gone (`meta.invSeen` is no longer used). Tests: `tests/ancient-test.js` (46), `tests/artifacts-test.js` (111).

## Artifacts in battle (v341)
`_backup/artpeek_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_artpeek.py` before the boot line, on top of v340. Simon: see which artifacts you have equipped during a run, without changing them. `artHudSync()` (from `updHud` and `showOverlay` wrappers, signature-checked in `APK.sig`) shows `#artHud` under the top bar on the right during a run when the battle hero (`heroU`, else `meta.equipped`) has at least one artifact in an open socket: rarity-framed icons with the level. Tapping it (or the hero sheet's `.apk-row`, added by `artSheetRow` into `heroSheetHtml`, whose signature now includes `ART_V`) opens `artPeekOpen()`: a read-only card (z-index 60, pauses the run and restores the pause on close) with each socket's artifact, rarity, element, level and current text, empty sockets and how locked ones open. Tests: `tests/artifacts-test.js` (118).

## Event cards (v342)
`_backup/evcard_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_evcard.py` before the boot line, on top of v341. Simon: tapping an event (Starfall, Blood Moon) should show its effects under it. `#bmPill` and `#sfPill` take taps now (`pointer-events:auto`, a chevron); `evcPill(k)` (from `bmPill`/`sfPill` wrappers) wires the tap and keeps an open card current. `evcOpen(k)` drops `#evCard` under the pill (z-index 7, themed crimson or indigo): `EVC_D[k]` effect rows with small SVG icons, a segment bar of the waves left, and the event's secret scrolls from `EVW[ev]` (found ones named, the rest '???', as in the Spellbook). It closes on a second tap, its x, the event ending or a new run (`resetRun` wrapper). The game keeps running. Tests: `tests/dev-test.js` (57).

## Cannon Bastion masteries (v343)
